#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate options
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, numOptions);
    
    // Allocate results
    std::vector<double> results(numOptions);
    
    // Price options
    printf("Pricing options...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        results[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::microseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration.count() / 1000.0);
    printf("Options per second: %.0f\n", numOptions / (duration.count() / 1e6));
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating results...\n");
        bool valid = validateResults(options, results);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

codex
The computation is embarrassingly parallel, so the scalable path is to generate each rank’s contiguous global slice locally—avoiding both input broadcast and root-side duplication—and time the slowest rank after a synchronization point. Full results only need to return to rank 0 for `-r`; validation needs at most the first ten values, so its communication can stay constant-size.

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(localRange.begin));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(validationCount == 0 ? 0 :
+                                                  localRange.begin + localRange.count,
+                                                  r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(localRange.begin));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(validationCount == 0 ? 0 :
+                                                  localRange.begin + localRange.count,
+                                                  r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT,
+               rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == root) {
+        displacements[0] = 0;
+        for (int r = 1; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = displacements[r - 1] + counts[r - 1];
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
 bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,71 +271,125 @@

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT,
+               rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == root) {
+        displacements[0] = 0;
+        for (int r = 1; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = displacements[r - 1] + counts[r - 1];
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT,
+               rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr, 1, MPI_INT,
+               root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == root) {
+        displacements[0] = 0;
+        for (int r = 1; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            displacements[r] = displacements[r - 1] + counts[r - 1];
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t total,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t total,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t total,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,71 +273,126 @@

+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+

+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT_2
#define M_SQRT_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_SQRT1_2
#define M_SQRT1_2 0.7071067811865475244
#endif
#ifndef M_1_SQRTPI
#define M_1_SQRTPI 0.564189583547756286948
#endif

enum OptionType {
    CALL = 0,
    PUT = 1
};

struct OptionInput {
    int type;           // CALL or PUT
    double strike;      // Strike price
    double spot;        // Spot price
    double q;           // Dividend yield
    double r;           // Risk-free rate
    double t;           // Time to maturity
    double vol;         // Volatility
    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)
    double tol;         // Tolerance
};

// Standard normal cumulative distribution function
inline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {
    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
}

// Standard normal probability density function
inline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {
    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);
}

// Black-Scholes formula for European options
double blackScholes(const OptionInput& option) noexcept {
    const double S = option.spot;
    const double K = option.strike;
    const double r = option.r;
    const double q = option.q;
    const double T = option.t;
    const double sigma = option.vol;
    
    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));
    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);
    
    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);
    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);
    const double discount = exp(-r * T);
    
    double price;
    if (option.type == CALL) {
        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;
    } else { // PUT
        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);
    }
    
    return price;
}

// Standard test cases for validation
inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
    return {{
        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},
        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},
    }};
}

// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions,
                     const size_t firstOption = 0) {
    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
    options.resize(numOptions);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
        const size_t globalIndex = firstOption + i;

        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[globalIndex % testOptions.size()];
        options[i] = base;
        
        // Add some variation for larger datasets
        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 *
            (globalIndex / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
        options[i].spot *= factor;
        options[i].strike *= factor;
    }
}

struct WorkRange {
    size_t begin;
    size_t count;
};

// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
    const size_t base = total / ranks;
    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
    return {begin, count};
}

// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
                      const WorkRange localRange,
                      const size_t total,
                      const int rank,
                      const int numRanks,
                      std::vector<double>& results) {
    constexpr int root = 0;

    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
        std::vector<int> counts;
        std::vector<int> displacements;
        if (rank == root) {
            results.resize(total);
            counts.resize(numRanks);
            displacements.resize(numRanks);
            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
            }
        }

        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        return;
    }

    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    if (rank == root) {
        results.resize(total);
        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
                  results.data() + localRange.begin);

        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
            size_t received = 0;
            while (received < range.count) {
                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
                received += chunk;
            }
        }
    } else {
        size_t sent = 0;
        while (sent < localRange.count) {
            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
            sent += chunk;
        }
    }
}

// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
                             const WorkRange localRange,
                             const size_t total,
                             const size_t validationCount,
                             const int rank,
                             const int numRanks,
                             std::vector<double>& results) {
    constexpr int root = 0;
    int localCount = 0;
    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
        localCount = static_cast<int>(
            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
    }

    std::vector<int> counts;
    std::vector<int> displacements;
    if (rank == root) {
        results.resize(validationCount);
        counts.resize(numRanks);
        displacements.resize(numRanks);
        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
            } else {
                counts[r] = 0;
                displacements[r] = 0;
            }
        }
    }

    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
}

bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
    bool allPassed = true;
    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
    
    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
        const double computed = results[i];
        const double expected = options[i].value;
        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
        
        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
               i, computed, expected, relError);
        
        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
            allPassed = false;
        }
    }
    
    return allPassed;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    if (MPI_Init(&argc, &argv) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
        return 1;
    }

    int rank = 0;
    int numRanks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);

    constexpr int root = 0;
    size_t numOptions = 10000;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    int parseStatus = 0;
    
    // Parse once so diagnostics and usage text are never duplicated by ranks.
    if (rank == root) {
        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
                numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
                validate = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
                printResults = true;
            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
                printUsage(argv[0]);
                parseStatus = 2;
                break;
            } else {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
                parseStatus = 1;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    MPI_Bcast(&parseStatus, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (parseStatus != 0) {
        MPI_Finalize();
        return parseStatus == 2 ? 0 : 1;
    }

    static_assert(sizeof(size_t) <= sizeof(unsigned long long));
    unsigned long long optionCount = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numOptions);
    int flags = (validate ? 1 : 0) | (printResults ? 2 : 0);
    MPI_Bcast(&optionCount, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Bcast(&flags, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    numOptions = static_cast<size_t>(optionCount);
    validate = (flags & 1) != 0;
    printResults = (flags & 2) != 0;
    
    if (rank == root) {
        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }
    
    // Every rank generates only its own deterministic slice. No input data is
    // replicated or communicated.
    const WorkRange localRange = partitionWork(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
    generateOptions(options, localRange.count, localRange.begin);
    
    std::vector<double> localResults(localRange.count);
    
    if (rank == root) {
        printf("Pricing options...\n");
    }
    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRange.count; ++i) {
        localResults[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
    }
    
    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, root,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == root) {
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n",
               duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
    }

    std::vector<double> results;
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        gatherAllResults(localResults, localRange, numOptions, rank, numRanks,
                         results);
        if (rank == root) {
            print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
        }
    }
    
    // Validation
    int exitCode = 0;
    if (validate) {
        constexpr size_t maxValidationOptions = 10;
        const size_t validationCount = std::min(maxValidationOptions, numOptions);
        if (!printResults) {
            gatherValidationResults(localResults, localRange, numOptions,
                                    validationCount,
                                    rank, numRanks, results);
        }
        
        if (rank == root) {
            std::vector<OptionInput> validationOptions;
            generateOptions(validationOptions, validationCount);
            printf("Validating results...\n");
            const bool valid = validateResults(validationOptions, results);
            printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
            exitCode = valid ? 0 : 1;
        }
        MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }
    
    MPI_Finalize();
    return exitCode;
}

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t total,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+
 bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
                      const std::vector<double>& results) {
     bool allPassed = true;
@@ -144,71 +273,126 @@
