+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t total,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}

   155	            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
   156	                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
   157	                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
   158	                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
   159	            }
   160	        }
   161	
   162	        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
   163	                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
   164	                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   165	                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   166	                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   167	        return;
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
   171	    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   172	    if (rank == root) {
   173	        results.resize(total);
   174	        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
   175	                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
   176	
   177	        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
   178	            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
   179	            size_t received = 0;
   180	            while (received < range.count) {
   181	                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
   182	                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
   183	                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
   184	                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   185	                received += chunk;
   186	            }
   187	        }
   188	    } else {
   189	        size_t sent = 0;
   190	        while (sent < localRange.count) {
   191	            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
   192	            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
   193	                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   194	            sent += chunk;
   195	        }
   196	    }
   197	}
   198	
   199	// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
   200	// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
   201	void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
   202	                             const WorkRange localRange,
   203	                             const size_t total,
   204	                             const size_t validationCount,
   205	                             const int rank,
   206	                             const int numRanks,
   207	                             std::vector<double>& results) {
   208	    constexpr int root = 0;
   209	    int localCount = 0;
   210	    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
   211	        localCount = static_cast<int>(
   212	            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    std::vector<int> counts;
   216	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   217	    if (rank == root) {
   218	        results.resize(validationCount);
   219	        counts.resize(numRanks);
   220	        displacements.resize(numRanks);
   221	        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
   222	            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
   223	            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
   224	                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
   225	                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
   226	                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
   227	            } else {
   228	                counts[r] = 0;
   229	                displacements[r] = 0;
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
   235	                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
   236	                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   237	                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   238	                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   239	}
   240	
   241	bool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, 
   242	                     const std::vector<double>& results) {
   243	    bool allPassed = true;
   244	    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());
   245	    
   246	    printf("Checking computed option prices:\n");
   247	    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {
   248	        const double computed = results[i];
   249	        const double expected = options[i].value;
   250	        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);
   251	        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);
   252	        
   253	        printf("  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\n", 
   254	               i, computed, expected, relError);
   255	        
   256	        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable
   257	        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {
   258	            printf("Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\n", i, computed);
   259	            allPassed = false;
   260	        }
   261	    }
   262	    
   263	    return allPassed;
   264	}
   265	
   266	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   267	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   268	    printf("Options:\n");
   269	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\n");
   270	    printf("  -v           Enable validation against known values\n");
   271	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   272	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   273	}
   274	
   275	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   276	    if (MPI_Init(&argc, &argv) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   277	        return 1;
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    int rank = 0;
   281	    int numRanks = 1;
   282	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   283	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);
   284	
   285	    constexpr int root = 0;
   286	    size_t numOptions = 10000;
   287	    bool validate = false;
   288	    bool printResults = false;
   289	    int parseStatus = 0;
   290	    
   291	    // Parse once so diagnostics and usage text are never duplicated by ranks.
   292	    if (rank == root) {
   293	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   294	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   295	                numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);
   296	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   297	                validate = true;
   298	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   299	                printResults = true;
   300	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   301	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   302	                parseStatus = 2;
   303	                break;
   304	            } else {
   305	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   306	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   307	                parseStatus = 1;
   308	                break;
   309	            }
   310	        }
   311	    }
   312	
   313	    MPI_Bcast(&parseStatus, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   314	    if (parseStatus != 0) {
   315	        MPI_Finalize();
   316	        return parseStatus == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   317	    }
   318	
   319	    static_assert(sizeof(size_t) <= sizeof(unsigned long long));
   320	    unsigned long long optionCount = static_cast<unsigned long long>(numOptions);
   321	    int flags = (validate ? 1 : 0) | (printResults ? 2 : 0);
   322	    MPI_Bcast(&optionCount, 1, MPI_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   323	    MPI_Bcast(&flags, 1, MPI_INT, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   324	    numOptions = static_cast<size_t>(optionCount);
   325	    validate = (flags & 1) != 0;
   326	    printResults = (flags & 2) != 0;
   327	    
   328	    if (rank == root) {
   329	        printf("Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\n");
   330	        printf("Number of options: %zu\n", numOptions);
   331	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   332	    }
   333	    
   334	    // Every rank generates only its own deterministic slice. No input data is
   335	    // replicated or communicated.
   336	    const WorkRange localRange = partitionWork(numOptions, rank, numRanks);
   337	    std::vector<OptionInput> options;
   338	    generateOptions(options, localRange.count, localRange.begin);
   339	    
   340	    std::vector<double> localResults(localRange.count);
   341	    
   342	    if (rank == root) {
   343	        printf("Pricing options...\n");
   344	    }
   345	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   346	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   347	    
   348	    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRange.count; ++i) {
   349	        localResults[i] = blackScholes(options[i]);
   350	    }
   351	    
   352	    const double localDuration = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   353	    double duration = 0.0;
   354	    MPI_Reduce(&localDuration, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, root,
   355	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   356	
   357	    if (rank == root) {
   358	        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", duration * 1000.0);
   359	        printf("Options per second: %.0f\n",
   360	               duration > 0.0 ? numOptions / duration : 0.0);
   361	    }
   362	
   363	    std::vector<double> results;
   364	    
   365	    // Print results for external validation
   366	    if (printResults) {
   367	        gatherAllResults(localResults, localRange, numOptions, rank, numRanks,
   368	                         results);
   369	        if (rank == root) {
   370	            print_results(results, "OptionPrices");
   371	        }
   372	    }
   373	    
   374	    // Validation
   375	    int exitCode = 0;
   376	    if (validate) {
   377	        constexpr size_t maxValidationOptions = 10;
   378	        const size_t validationCount = std::min(maxValidationOptions, numOptions);
   379	        if (!printResults) {
   380	            gatherValidationResults(localResults, localRange, numOptions,
   381	                                    validationCount,
   382	                                    rank, numRanks, results);
   383	        }
   384	        
   385	        if (rank == root) {
   386	            std::vector<OptionInput> validationOptions;
   387	            generateOptions(validationOptions, validationCount);
   388	            printf("Validating results...\n");
   389	            const bool valid = validateResults(validationOptions, results);
   390	            printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.

+                             const int numRanks,

+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI parallelization while preserving the `black_scholes` executable name.

Key changes:

- Balanced contiguous work distribution with rank-local option generation.
- Timed computation uses `MPI_Wtime` and reports the slowest rank.
- No result gathering during normal benchmark runs.
- Ordered `MPI_Gatherv` only for `-r`; validation gathers at most ten values.
- Mandatory MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/black-scholes_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- MPI implementation is in [black_scholes.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/black-scholes_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp:119).

Verification:

- Original and 4-rank output hashes match exactly: `a1819ec14da95077`.
- Validation passes with uneven partitions, empty input, and more ranks than options.
- Local 2,000,000-option benchmark:
  - 1 rank: 180.3 ms
  - 2 ranks: 89.9 ms
  - 4 ranks: 45.1 ms (~4.0× speedup)

+struct WorkRange {
+    size_t begin;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+// Assign contiguous, nearly equal-sized ranges. Contiguous ranges preserve the
+// original result order and make the optional result collection inexpensive.
+WorkRange partitionWork(const size_t total, const int rank, const int numRanks) noexcept {
+    const size_t ranks = static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);
+    const size_t rankIndex = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    const size_t base = total / ranks;
+    const size_t remainder = total % ranks;
+    const size_t count = base + (rankIndex < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+    const size_t begin = rankIndex * base + std::min(rankIndex, remainder);
+    return {begin, count};
+}
+
+// Collect the complete ordered result only when external result printing was
+// requested. The normal benchmark path therefore has no result-collection
+// traffic. MPI_Gatherv is fastest for normal-sized inputs; the chunked fallback
+// also supports vectors larger than MPI's traditional signed-int count limit.
+void gatherAllResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                      const WorkRange localRange,
+                      const size_t total,
+                      const int rank,
+                      const int numRanks,
+                      std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+
+    if (total <= static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == root) {
+            results.resize(total);
+            counts.resize(numRanks);
+            displacements.resize(numRanks);
+            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+                const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(range.count);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), static_cast<int>(localRange.count), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                    MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    constexpr int gatherTag = 1;
+    const size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(total);
+        std::copy(localResults.begin(), localResults.end(),
+                  results.data() + localRange.begin);
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < numRanks; ++source) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, source, numRanks);
+            size_t received = 0;
+            while (received < range.count) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, range.count - received);
+                MPI_Recv(results.data() + range.begin + received,
+                         static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, source, gatherTag,
+                         MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                received += chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t sent = 0;
+        while (sent < localRange.count) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(maxChunk, localRange.count - sent);
+            MPI_Send(localResults.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                     root, gatherTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            sent += chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Validation inspects only the first ten options. Gather just that prefix
+// instead of forcing all computed prices through rank 0.
+void gatherValidationResults(const std::vector<double>& localResults,
+                             const WorkRange localRange,
+                             const size_t total,
+                             const size_t validationCount,
+                             const int rank,
+                             const int numRanks,
+                             std::vector<double>& results) {
+    constexpr int root = 0;
+    int localCount = 0;
+    if (localRange.begin < validationCount) {
+        localCount = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min(localRange.count, validationCount - localRange.begin));
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == root) {
+        results.resize(validationCount);
+        counts.resize(numRanks);
+        displacements.resize(numRanks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {
+            const WorkRange range = partitionWork(total, r, numRanks);
+            if (range.begin < validationCount) {
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min(range.count, validationCount - range.begin));
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(range.begin);
+            } else {
+                counts[r] = 0;
+                displacements[r] = 0;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localResults.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == root ? results.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == root ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, root, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+}
+

     bool allPassed = true;
